Can a learned map transfer calibration effects?

One fixed ridge model uses only the original 272 training profiles and measured targets. No development fitting, threshold changes or reserved-test evaluation. Original 0.02 no-change floor retained.

This method scores the actual update on a fixed library with the base model. It adds a learned profile map; it is not an improved learned LoRAcle checkpoint.
